+    if (gMpiRank == 0) printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    const size_t first = state.numTriangles * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / ranks;
+    const size_t last = state.numTriangles * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / ranks;

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* __restrict__ out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* __restrict__ out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* __restrict__ out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* __restrict__ out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

@@ -44,6 +59,50 @@
 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

+void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state, int rank, int ranks) {

+    const size_t last = state.numTriangles * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / ranks;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    }
+}
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) cudaCheck((call), #call)

@@ -44,6 +59,50 @@
 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

codex
Implemented and validated the hybrid accelerator-cluster version.

Changed only:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_hybrid_r2/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_hybrid_r2/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)

Parallelization:

- MPI distributes form-factor and time-delay matrix rows.
- OpenMP parallelizes per-row host computations.
- CUDA accelerates wave propagation and cross-correlation.
- Executable remains `roomsim`.
- No new external dependencies.

Validation passed with both one and two MPI ranks, including CUDA execution and built-in correctness checks.

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<int>(error));
+    }
+}

@@ -44,6 +59,50 @@
 constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
 constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;
 
+// Dense stages run on the accelerator. One launch per timestep preserves the
+// causal ordering of the original recurrence while exposing all triangles.
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ kij,
+                                const int* __restrict__ tau,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ areas,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ rho,
+                                const val_t* __restrict__ radE,
+                                const val_t* radB,
+                                val_t* out,
+                                size_t n, size_t t) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t sum = 0.0f;
+    const size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (delay <= static_cast<int>(t)) {
+            val_t k = kij[row + j];
+            if (k > 0.0f) {
+                val_t source = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+                if (source > 0.0f) sum += fminf(k * areas[j], 1.0f) * source;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    out[t * n + i] = rho[i] * sum + radE[t * n + i];
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* __restrict__ radB,
+                               val_t* __restrict__ distances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t source) {
+    size_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n) return;
+    val_t best = 0.0f;
+    int bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = 0.0f;
+        for (size_t tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + source] * radB[(tt - t) * n + i];
+        if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestT = static_cast<int>(t); }
+    }
+    distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+

Parallelization:

- MPI distributes form-factor and time-delay matrix rows.
- OpenMP parallelizes per-row host computations.
- CUDA accelerates wave propagation and cross-correlation.
- Executable remains `roomsim`.
- No new external dependencies.

Validation passed with both one and two MPI ranks, including CUDA execution and built-in correctness checks.
